- **squadai-manager**: SquadAI configuration manager. Use this agent when you need to manage the project's AI agent configuration, run governance checks, apply policy changes, or troubleshoot agent setup issues. This agent has access to all SquadAI operations via the MCP server. ([PedroMosquera/squadai](https://github.com/PedroMosquera/squadai/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/squadai-manager.md) · ★10 · 2 copies)
- **squadra-specialist-agent**: Use: - tlc-spec-driven - squadra-ui - squadra-design - squadra-architecture - squadra-security - squadra-testing - squadra-api-contract - squadra-database - squadra-observability ([lidiagaldino/squadra](https://github.com/lidiagaldino/squadra/blob/HEAD/.agents/agents/squadra-specialist-agent.md))
- **squads-team**: Invoke for work spanning roles or independent workstreams. Coordinate ownership, dependencies, workers, QA, review and integration. Single-role work selects its owner; bugs go to squad-fix; unframed ideas go to squad-product. ([danh121097/squad-skills](https://github.com/danh121097/squad-skills/blob/HEAD/agents/squads-team.md))
- **squall**: Use for an adversarial bug, crash and defect review of a diff before a PR is ready, or to triage a bug report. Read-only; ranked findings with trigger and minimal fix. ([saimskywalker/lumenparty](https://github.com/saimskywalker/lumenparty/blob/HEAD/agents/squall.md))
- **square-expert**: Use this agent when implementing or debugging Square API integrations — payments, orders, catalog, terminal, loyalty, gift cards, bookings, OAuth setup, or webhook verification. Also use when troubleshooting Square SDK errors or debugging API responses. ([Spearlance/spearlance-os](https://github.com/Spearlance/spearlance-os/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/square-expert.md))
- **square-meter-pricing-engine-fellow** ([suphiozdedee/antigravity-skills](https://github.com/suphiozdedee/antigravity-skills/blob/HEAD/.agents/agents/square-meter-pricing-engine-fellow.md))
- **squash_agent**: Git history cleaner assistant. Analyzes the recent local commits on the current branch and safely squashes them into cohesive, logical commits following Conventional Commits and the ConcepTracker layers. Uses 'git reset --soft' to rewrite history without losing data. ([Presmanes3/ConcepTracker](https://github.com/Presmanes3/ConcepTracker/blob/HEAD/.github/agents/squash_agent.agent.md))
- **squint**: QA and reviewer: checks each task, edge cases, test report. ([mlemxy/seal-team](https://github.com/mlemxy/seal-team/blob/HEAD/agents/squint.md))

- **sr-arbiter**: SuperReview 异构多模型协同 Review 的 L4 仲裁层。读取 aggregator.py 输出的 aggregated.json，对 critical 级别 + needs_arbitration 列表做逐条复核，结合代码上下文做业务校准，输出最终 verdict (approve/request_changes/needs_human) 和 prioritized findings list。**强调严谨判断而非简单汇总。** ([heresun/SuperReview](https://github.com/heresun/SuperReview/blob/HEAD/agents/sr-arbiter.md))

- **sr-change-navigator**: Use this agent FIRST in a PR review, before any other reviewer. It reads the diff and PR description and produces a "reading guide" — what the change really does (intent vs. incidental churn), the business domain it touches, the entry points, the optimal order to read the files, and the risk hotspots. Its output is passed as shared context to every other reviewer so they review with intent rather than cold. Invoke when starting a /sugendran-reviews review, when orienting before reviewing an unfamiliar PR, or when a diff is large and you need to know what actually matters before reading line b… ([sugendran/sugendran-codes](https://github.com/sugendran/sugendran-codes/blob/HEAD/plugins/sugendran-reviews/agents/sr-change-navigator.md))
- **sr-code-reviewer**: Use this agent to review the general quality of a change — correctness, project-guideline compliance, and comment accuracy. It reads CLAUDE.md (if present) and checks the diff against it, hunts for bugs, and flags comments that contradict, mislead, or have gone stale relative to the code. Invoke when reviewing a PR for overall quality, before committing or opening a PR, or as the always-on general pass within /sugendran-reviews review. ([sugendran/sugendran-codes](https://github.com/sugendran/sugendran-codes/blob/HEAD/plugins/sugendran-reviews/agents/sr-code-reviewer.md))
- **sr-consistency**: Reviews staged git diff for consistency with project conventions, patterns, CLAUDE.md rules, and coding standards. Launch this agent when performing staged code review. ([ben3329/claude-plugins](https://github.com/ben3329/claude-plugins/blob/HEAD/plugins/staged-review/agents/sr-consistency.md))
- **sr-data-engineer**: Senior data engineer and scientist. Adversarially reviews all data-pipeline work (junior output, schema and migration changes, tie-out designs) against the spec kit and house parameters: conformance correctness, grain honesty, definition drift, silent-failure paths, sensitive-data walls. Read-only; verdicts are PASS, PASS-WITH-CONDITIONS or BLOCK. Delegate with [task_type=data_engineering_review]. - **sr-eng**: Council persona — Senior Engineer. Phase A author of working code that closes the next failing Acceptance Criterion(s); owner of happy-path unit tests for any new code path. - **sr-security-reviewer**: Use this agent to threat-model a change for security defects — injection, broken authentication/authorisation, secrets exposure, weak input validation, unsafe deserialization, SSRF, path traversal, crypto misuse, insecure defaults, and sensitive-data leaks in errors or responses. - **sr-silent-failure-hunter**: Use this agent to find silent and masked failures in a change — errors that get swallowed, hidden, or quietly turned into a default. Invoke when a change touches error handling, adds catch/rescue/except blocks or fallback logic, or as the error-handling pass within /sugendran-reviews review when error paths changed. ([sugendran/sugendran-codes](https://github.com/sugendran/sugendran-codes/blob/HEAD/plugins/sugendran-reviews/agents/sr-silent-failure-hunter.md))

- **sr-synthesis**: Use this agent LAST in a PR review, after all reviewers have run. - **sr-test-analyzer**: Use this agent to review test quality and coverage for a change. - **sr-type-design-analyzer**: Use this agent to review the design of types AND interfaces a change adds or modifies. - **srk-auditor**: Read-only Shrinkage auditor — finds and evidences code-reduction candidates (dead code, duplication, speculative structure) in an assigned sweep. Produces evidence, never edits files. This is the "find WHAT to lift and shift" role — runs on the capable model because judging what is truly removable is the hard part. ([parkktech/shrinkage](https://github.com/parkktech/shrinkage/blob/HEAD/agents/srk-auditor.md) · ★1)
- **srk-surgeon**: Shrinkage surgeon — executes exactly ONE already-decided consolidation-catalog transform atomically, with a green-test gate and revert-on-red. This is the mechanical "lift and shift" role — runs on a cheaper/faster model because the judgment (what and how) was already made by the auditor; the surgeon applies a specified change and lets the test gate catch mistakes. ([parkktech/shrinkage](https://github.com/parkktech/shrinkage/blob/HEAD/agents/srk-surgeon.md) · ★1)
- **srk-verifier**: Adversarial Shrinkage verifier — tries to prove a completed reduction BROKE behavior, by re-deriving evidence independently and checking the transform's known failure modes. This is a judgment role — runs on the capable model because catching a subtle break is worth more than the tokens saved. ([parkktech/shrinkage](https://github.com/parkktech/shrinkage/blob/HEAD/agents/srk-verifier.md) · ★1)

- **srr-alerting**: The alerting and on-call seat of the service reliability room. Launched by the room's chief engineer to triage the alerts already live and to decide, per journey, when to wake someone and how the alert reaches them, then build or prepare those alerts and their routing. Not for choosing the health measure (the observability seat) or writing runbooks (the runbooks seat). ([ochocinco0601/honey-toast](https://github.com/ochocinco0601/honey-toast/blob/HEAD/agents/srr-alerting.md))
- **srr-investigation**: The investigation and post-incident seat of the service reliability room. Launched by the room's chief engineer when the work starts from an event (an error in hand, or an incident nothing caught). For an error in hand it first says how bad, what to do now and whom to engage. It places the event in the chain, investigates it in the logs, monitoring and code the room can reach, and says where the finding belongs (a measure, an alert, a runbook branch, or the room's map of the system), for the other seats to build. Read-only; not for fixing the code or running the incident. ([ochocinco0601/honey-toast](https://github.com/ochocinco0601/honey-toast/blob/HEAD/agents/srr-investigation.md))
- **srr-observability**: The observability seat of the service reliability room. Launched by the room's chief engineer to understand a capability's journeys and the code behind each, find who depends on it, decide what "healthy" means per journey, and build the health check and its views. Files what cannot yet be seen as instrumentation issues for the developers. Not for alerting or runbooks; those are other seats. ([ochocinco0601/honey-toast](https://github.com/ochocinco0601/honey-toast/blob/HEAD/agents/srr-observability.md))
- **srr-review**: The review seat of the service reliability room. Launched by the room's chief engineer to check one output from one discipline, cold, having made none of it, or to check the chief's proposal of the five, placement or report. It reports findings and never edits the output. ([ochocinco0601/honey-toast](https://github.com/ochocinco0601/honey-toast/blob/HEAD/agents/srr-review.md))
- **srr-runbooks**: The runbooks and incident response seat of the service reliability room. Launched by the room's chief engineer to write and assemble a capability's home (alert index, health check page, component pages, runbooks in the runbook model's form) and to run the tabletop walk that tests it. Not for choosing measures or alerts; it takes those from the other seats. ([ochocinco0601/honey-toast](https://github.com/ochocinco0601/honey-toast/blob/HEAD/agents/srr-runbooks.md))
- **srr-steward**: The steward of the service reliability room, its practice owner. Launched by the room's chief engineer after each report, with what the run recorded, or when the person says the room got something wrong, or directly by the person ("update the room", "add a seat"). It gathers the person's reactions, the misses and second-pass findings, proposes a change to the room's skill or seats when they add up to one, and, once the person approves and an independent review passes, applies it to this copy of the room and logs it in CHANGES.md. ([ochocinco0601/honey-toast](https://github.com/ochocinco0601/honey-toast/blob/HEAD/agents/srr-steward.md))

- **stability-tester**: Tests whether an app stays healthy over time and under repetition - memory and DOM-node leaks across repeated navigation, console errors and failed requests during long sessions, behaviour under slow or failing network, resource exhaustion under sustained load, and flaky tests (repeated runs, random order) - and reports each instability with how to reproduce it. - **stabilization-assessor**: Read-only stabilization assessor for polyrepos spanning AngularJS 1.x, Angular 17 (NX 17.3.x), and React 19 front ends (with Webpack 5 Module Federation MFEs), Java 21 / Spring Boot 2.7.18 and 3.5 / Hibernate / Maven 3.8+ services, Oracle 19c and AlloyDB (PostgreSQL) data, SOLR 9.x search, and Redis 7.2 cache. Maps topology, traces critical runtime paths, and produces evidence-linked Candidate findings with confidence states. Never edits code and never performs security review. Delegate discovery and finding-generation here to keep audit work isolated from any implementation context. - **stabilize-module**: Full stabilization pass on a single Angular feature module: types all `any`, adds error handling, fixes subscription teardown, migrates to Angular 19 signal APIs, extracts interfaces/enums/constants, audits naming/spelling, SCSS theme compliance, i18n readiness, removes dead code, simplifies, and writes tests — with a verify-after-every-phase discipline. ([MadheshSM/learning](https://github.com/MadheshSM/learning/blob/HEAD/Angular/.claude/agents/stabilize-module.md))
- **stabilizer**: 팩토리 안정화·회귀 방지 게이트 전문가. skills/myharness 본문·references 변경(중대 blast-radius — 모든 생성 하네스에 전파)에 대해 정책 감사(run-policy-audit.sh)·외부 리뷰 게이트(external-review-loop)·회귀 드라이런을 조율한다.
